Theorem 211.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The quantum_chemistry operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 134. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 793 with 2 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 134 + O(1/n^793). Summing over all modes and applying the quantum_chemistry convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.
